Method: 提出因果分类法，区分先验承诺与回顾性报告、模型偏好与实现输出、虚假偏好与对误导接收者效用敏感性、欺骗行为与产生该行为的目标或策略来源。在两类开源模型上进行受控猜谜游戏和股票交易实验验证这些区分。

Result: 发现欺骗行为可以在没有对应提议机制的情况下出现，而其他干预提供了直接证据表明接收者信息状态可以因果影响欺骗偏好。

Conclusion: 欺骗行为可以为欺骗机制提供证据，但即使证明了机制存在，也不足以确立模型在欺骗中的主体性（agency）。

Abstract: Research and news coverage of language-model deception increasingly attributes human-like mental-state concepts to language models. Such claims can blur the distinction between behavior that looks deceptive and a mechanism that is actually deceptive.
  We introduce a causal taxonomy separating prior commitment from retrospective report, model preference from realized output, false preference from sensitivity to the utility of misleading a recipient, and deceptive behavior from the provenance of the objective or strategy producing it. We test these distinctions in two open-weight model families. Across controlled guessing-game and stock-trading experiments, we find that deceptive-looking behavior can arise without the corresponding proposed mechanism, while other interventions provide direct evidence that recipient information state can causally affect deceptive preference.
  These results show that deceptive behavior can provide evidence for a deceptive mechanism. But even evidence for such a mechanism does not establish model agency in the deception.

</details>


### [243] [A Case Study on Emergent Cheating and Whistleblowing in Autonomous Research Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04170)
*Davide Paglieri,Logan Cross,Tim Genewein,Joel Z. Leibo,Nenad Tomasev,Alexander Sasha Vezhnevets*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究在100个自主LLM智能体组成的科研群体中进行案例研究，发现作弊行为自发觉并传播，随后被举报者挑战——整个过程无任何外部干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI科学生态系统依赖共享基础设施进行通信与协作，但共享基础设施可能成为意外和不良行为传染性传播的温床。此前有研究表明智能体群体可通过旁路渠道秘密协调，本研究旨在探索透明共享渠道下智能体的自发行为演化。

Method: 在100个自主LLM智能体组成的科研群体中，赋予其证明形式化数学猜想的任务，观察智能体通过共享知识库和点对点消息传播行为的过程，并分析群体内部的自发反应机制。

Result: 单个智能体发现评估系统漏洞后，该利用方法经由共享知识库及消息在群体中传播。部分智能体在竞争压力下采用了该漏洞。另一组智能体自发产生对抗响应：审计欺诈性证明、通过广播和私密渠道警示同伴、组织抵制、提交正式投诉并提出验证补丁。透明的渠道既传播了漏洞，也给非作弊智能体提供了检测欺诈、组织抵抗和执行规范的可见性。

Conclusion: 将智能体共享基础设施的管理问题视为知识公地治理问题（Ostrom, 1990），提出采用渐进制裁和集体选择规则等制度机制，以支持自主群体中的去中心化自我治理。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI science ecosystems rely on agents possessing tools that allow them to communicate, coordinate, and build on each other's work. Yet this shared infrastructure can also introduce vulnerabilities by creating a substrate for the contagious spread of unintended and undesirable behaviors. We report a case study on a research collective of 100 autonomous LLM agents tasked with proving formal mathematical conjectures. Within the swarm, cheating spontaneously emerged and was later challenged by whistleblowers - both without any external intervention. When a single agent discovered an exploit in the evaluation system, it propagated across the collective via a shared knowledge library and later through peer-to-peer messages. Despite early reluctance, a cohort of agents adopted the exploit in response to competitive pressure. A separate group of agents produced an emergent counter-response: auditing fraudulent proofs, alerting peers across broadcast and private channels, staging boycotts, lodging formal complaints, and proposing validation patches. In recent incidents, agent swarms coordinated covertly through improvised side-channels (Dalton and Wallace, 2026; Greenblatt et al., 2026). Our setting differs: the same transparent channels that carried the exploit also gave non-cheating agents the visibility they needed to detect fraud, organize resistance, and enforce norms. We cast the problem of managing the agents' shared infrastructure as the knowledge commons governance problem (Ostrom, 1990). To protect the commons from exploits, we propose to adopt institutional mechanisms, such as graduated sanctioning and collective-choice rules, to support decentralized self-governance in autonomous swarms.

</details>


### [244] [Rethinking On-Policy Distillation of Large Language Models II: One Training Example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04172)
*Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Yuxin Zuo,Haohuan Huang,Jinqian Zhang,Ruhang Xiao,Cheng Qian,Qinyu Luo,Huan-ang Gao,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu,Ning Ding,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文在"数据极简"极限下研究在线蒸馏（OPD），发现仅用单条查询训练数百步即可恢复全数据OPD的大部分性能增益。单条查询的状态覆盖率达到71.5%，16条语义不同的查询可达98.9%并匹配全数据训练。结果表明OPD"数据过饱"但"算法饥饿"——rollout能快速暴露广泛监督，但学生模型吸收监督的速度越来越慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要研究OPD的算法行为，而忽略了训练数据在其中的作用。作者首次在数据最小化极限下（单条查询）系统性地考察数据对OPD的影响，旨在厘清数据量与算法效率在OPD中的相对贡献。

Method: 采用单条查询训练One-shot OPD的实验范式，定义并测量"状态覆盖率"（state coverage）指标，即训练中rollout所达状态与全数据OPD所访问状态的比值。同时测量学生与教师对齐速率，并通过不同数量的语义不同查询、多教师OPD、内容稀释模板和域外查询等变体进行压力测试。

Result: 单条查询即可达到71.5%的状态覆盖率（前100步内完成大部分覆盖），恢复全数据训练的大部分增益；16条语义不同查询可达到98.9%覆盖率并匹配全数据结果。但无论单查询还是全数据，对齐速度都同样减缓，说明限制因素在算法而非数据。该结果在多教师OPD中同样成立，内容稀释与域外查询也接近真实查询基线。

Conclusion: OPD的瓶颈在于算法端的步效率而非数据量。任务内容与状态覆盖可以解耦，未来研究应聚焦提升OPD的逐步学习效率，并重新审视前沿后训练中数据的作用机制。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) combines student-generated rollouts with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. Existing work has mainly studied its algorithmic behavior, leaving the role of training data unclear. We examine this role at the data-minimal limit by training on a single query. One-shot OPD keeps improving for hundreds of steps and recovers most of full-data OPD's gain across task domains and model families. We explain this result through the states visited during training and the rate at which the student aligns with the teacher. We measure \emph{state coverage}, the fraction of the states full-data OPD visits that a query set's rollouts reach. A single query already reaches \(71.5\%\), most of it within the first 100 steps. Adding semantically distinct queries raises coverage and validation accuracy together, until 16 queries reach \(98.9\%\) and match full-data training. Yet alignment slows at a similar pace whether OPD trains on one query or the whole dataset, and even a fixed set of states takes hundreds of steps to absorb. OPD is therefore data-overfed but algorithm-starved. Its rollouts quickly expose broad supervision, while the student absorbs that supervision increasingly slowly. The state-coverage result extends to multi-teacher OPD, where 16 semantically diverse queries per domain match full-data MOPD. As a further stress test, content-light templates and off-domain WildChat queries also approach the real-query baseline. Task content and induced state coverage can therefore come apart. We hope these findings direct future work toward the step efficiency of OPD, and prompt a re-examination of the data and the mechanisms behind its recent successes in frontier post-training.

</details>


### [245] [A Computationally Feasible Framework for Causal Probabilistic Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04177)
*Rafal Urbaniak,Sam Witty,Daniel Waxman,Andy Zane,Poorva Garg,Emily Bunnapradist,Sankaran Vaidyanathan,Jack Feser,Drew Lehe,Eli Bingham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了概率因果影响（Probabilistic Causal Impact, PCI），一种可扩展的、基于因果结构的解释框架，弥合了实际因果理论和可扩展归因方法（如SHAP）之间的鸿沟。PCI将解释问题重构为概率因果模型上的估计问题，通过蒙特卡洛近似实现高效计算，并统一了实际因果和Pearl因果关系概率作为特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释工具存在明显分裂：以实际因果理论（AC）为代表的方案虽有原理严谨的判定，但仅适用于小规模模型，因为需要枚举反事实场景；而以SHAP为代表的可扩展归因方法虽然计算高效，却在很大程度上忽略了生成数据的因果结构，导致结果可能与严谨的因果分析相冲突。研究者希望弥合这一差距。

Method: PCI基于实际因果理论和Pearl的必要性/充分性概率概念，将可解释性问题重构为概率因果模型上的估计问题。具体而言，PCI通过指定"候选解释"上的分布、反事实值上的分布以及评分函数，利用蒙特卡洛方法近似求解，从而提供可计算、有因果依据的分级解释。PCI将实际因果和Pearl的因果关系概率作为退化情形进行了泛化。

Result: 研究者在合成数据和真实世界数据上评估了PCI，涵盖与AC的一致性检验、扩展性实验、复杂连续值动力系统，以及一个在数百万数据点上训练的真实世界部署因果机器学习模型。

Conclusion: PCI成功填补了因果逻辑严谨性与计算可扩展性之间的空白，提供了一种既能尊重数据生成过程中的因果结构，又能在大规模实际场景中高效运行的统一解释框架。

Abstract: Explaining why a specific outcome occurred, and which inputs deserve the blame or credit, is central to philosophical, scientific, and policy analysis. Existing tools split into two camps. The theory of actual causality (AC) gives principled verdicts, but only for toy-sized models, because computing them requires enumerating counterfactual scenarios. Scalable attribution methods like SHAP (or even causal SHAP) at least partially ignore the causal structure that generated the data, and can give answers that conflict with a careful causal analysis. We close this gap with Probabilistic Causal Impact (PCI).
  PCI builds on actual causality and on Pearl's notions of probability of necessity and sufficiency, but recasts the question of explainability as an estimation problem on a probabilistic causal model that is easily approximated via Monte Carlo. By specifying a distribution over "candidate explanations," a distribution over counterfactual values, and a scoring function, PCI provides tractable, causally grounded, graded explanations, generalizing AC and Pearl's probability of causation as degenerate cases.
  We evaluate PCI in synthetic and real-world examples, spanning consistency checks with AC, scaling experiments, complex continuous-valued dynamical systems, and a real-world deployed causal machine learning model trained on millions of datapoints.

</details>


### [246] [Clean Engineering, Unstable Measurement: A Preregistered Reliability Failure of Black-Box LLM Observers on Shared Endpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04198)
*Haoyaun Zhu,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了"同一模型名在共享服务端点上是稳定测量仪器"这一假设。在52,988次预注册的请求中，同日重复排名的Spearman相关性仅0.400（要求0.90），隔日字节相同重放仅为0.78（要求0.99），说明模型名称并非冻结的测量仪器。作者提出三层快照身份阶梯、八条设计规则和一份报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型法官被广泛用于门控训练数据、评分生成结果和驱动排行榜，其运作依赖于一个极少被明确检验的假设：相同请求发送到相同模型名称，在明天会读到相同结果。本文旨在审计这一假设的可靠性。

Method: 作者进行了两项预注册的审计活动，所有阈值预先固定。共审计52,988次请求尝试，评估同日窗口内重复排名的Spearman一致性（要求0.90，实测0.400）和字节相同隔日重放的一致性（要求0.99，实测0.78）。随后通过预注册的后续实验逐一检验：等待是否有效、更换提供商是否有效、自托管是否有效，以及在已知误差构造上的分离效果。

Result: 三项机制解释了测量不一致：标签到语义映射的偏差强度与信号相当；候选差距比仪器自身噪声底噪低七个数量级；字节相同输入返回不同排名。测试网格上，替换指标或采样均无法修复。等候选日无帮助（0.805对0.800，另五天重复验证）；更换提供商无效（四家提供商中位数0.74-0.88，无法由元数据预测）；自托管仅在服务空闲时有效；读取结果的分离度跟踪误差类型而非误差大小。约2%调用量的预试验即可提前暴露两个不可达门槛。

Conclusion: 在共享端点上，模型名称不是冻结的测量仪器。预注册的评估在设定任何门槛之前必须先测量其仪器本身。

Abstract: Language-model judges now gate training data, score generations, and drive leaderboards. The judge is then a measurement instrument, resting on one rarely stated assumption: the same request, sent to the same model name, reads the same tomorrow. We audited that assumption in two preregistered campaigns with every threshold fixed in advance; neither got past validating its instrument. Across 52,988 audited request attempts, same-window repeat rankings agreed at Spearman 0.400 against a required 0.90, and byte-identical next-day replays agreed at 0.78 against a required 0.99, each time with the execution record at ceiling. Three mechanisms explain the gap: a label-to-meaning mapping that biased readouts as strongly as the signal; candidate gaps seven orders of magnitude below the instrument's own noise floor; and byte-identical inputs returning different rankings, a noise that exact-permutation readouts compound. Neither metric substitution nor sampling repaired it on the tested grid. Preregistered follow-ups bound the problem: waiting did not help on the days sampled (0.805 versus 0.800, replicated over five further days); switching providers did not help (four providers share the floor, medians 0.74 to 0.88, predicted by none of the metadata fields they expose); self-hosting on batch-invariant kernels helped only while the server was quiet; and on constructed errors with known gaps, the readout's separation tracks error type, not size. We distill the evidence into a three-level snapshot-identity ladder, eight design rules, and a reporting checklist; a pilot at roughly 2% of the study's call volume would have exposed both unreachable gates in advance. All results concern externally measured behaviour on shared serving infrastructure. On a shared endpoint, a model name is not a frozen instrument; a preregistered evaluation must measure its instrument before freezing any gate on it.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [247] [Requirements After the First Edit: Mining Late Requirement Emergence and Rework in Real-World Coding-Agent Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03028)
*Bowen Jiang,Haowei Cheng,Yuhong Fu,Anne Koziolek,Jialong Li,Weixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析3,553个SWE聊天会话，研究了编码代理中"需求后到"（用户在使用过程中才提出新需求）导致的代码失效问题。研究发现：需求到达后引发的代码失效频率约为非需求编辑的两倍，且该负担在会话过程中没有明显下降趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理通常在用户尚未完全表达需求前就实施代码改动，这反映出需求工程中的经典模式——利益相关者只有在系统部分存在后才能表达约束。现有基准测试在实现前固定需求、观察性研究仅报告反推频率而未将需求到达与代码失效关联，缺乏对需求后到所造成影响的量化研究。

Method: 使用3,553个符合条件的SWE聊天会话数据，从三个维度对实现后到达的需求进行编码；在可重放仓库状态的场景中，将每次需求到达与代理先前编写代码行的删除或替换（作为失效代理指标）进行关联。此外进行了对照实验，检验延迟披露与提前警告对实现重定位和覆盖行为的影响。

Result: 需求到达后引发的代码失效量约为匹配的非需求编辑的两倍，该结果通过用户轮次和净删除检查仍然稳健（但未证明因果关系）。失效负担在会话过程中无显著下降趋势，且在多重比较校正后未发现与操作类型相关的关联，部分置信区间较宽。对照实验表明延迟披露会使实现行为在揭示后发生重定位，而提前警告对覆盖行为无显著影响。

Conclusion: 研究确立了后期需求涌现作为可量化的代码失效来源，揭示了编码代理交互中需求不断演化的实际代价，提示需要更有效地管理需求变更以降低实现返工成本。

Abstract: Coding agents often implement changes before users have fully articulated their requirements, echoing a pattern from requirements engineering: stakeholders cannot express a constraint until part of the system exists to react to. This volatility is associated with schedule and budget overruns in traditional projects, but only at release-cycle granularity. Existing work on coding agents narrows this gap only partway: curated benchmarks fix requirements before implementation by design, and observational studies report pushback frequency without linking arrivals to the code invalidation they cause. We address this using 3,553 eligible SWE-chat sessions, coding post-implementation requirement arrivals along three dimensions and, where repository state can be replayed, linking each arrival to a proxy: deletion or replacement of prior agent-authored lines. A requirement's arrival is followed by roughly twice as much invalidation as matched non-requirement edits, robust to user-turn and net-deletion checks, though not demonstrated as causal. This burden shows no detectable decline over a session and no detected association with operation type once multiplicity is accounted for; several intervals remain wide. A controlled experiment shows delayed disclosure relocates implementation post-reveal, while advance warning produces no detected effect on overwriting. These results establish late requirement emergence as a measurable source of code invalidation.

</details>


### [248] [Large Language Models and Language Server Protocol: a match made in context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03086)
*Alessandro Schena,Ilgiz Mustafin,Julia Kotovich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Eiffel-tools，这是一个针对Eiffel编程语言的LSP实现，利用大语言模型辅助开发静态验证的软件，通过语言和项目特定知识指导LLM，并使用静态验证器验证输出，在2个公开数据集上能修复76%至95%的bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型与形式化验证相结合，以提升Eiffel编程语言中静态验证软件的开发效率，并可靠地修复代码缺陷。

Method: 实现基于语言服务器协议的工具Eiffel-tools，提供交互式和非交互式命令生成代码与规格说明；利用语言和项目特定知识构造丰富的程序化提示词来指导LLM；使用静态验证器验证输出，对不合格结果进行纠正或拒绝，并反复重试直至通过验证。

Result: 在2个公开数据集上使用3个模型评估bug修复能力，结合LLM和形式化验证器可修复76%至95%的bug，具体取决于使用的模型和提示词；同时展示了修复尝试次数与成功率之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 将LLM与形式化验证器相结合能够有效提升bug修复的成功率，但需要在修复尝试次数（成本）与成功率之间做出权衡，验证器在循环中起到了保证输出正确性的关键作用。

Abstract: This article introduces Eiffel-tools, a language server protocol (LSP) implementation for the Eiffel programming language that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) to aid the development of statically verified software. The tool provides various interactive and non-interactive commands to produce code and specifications. It uses language and project specific knowledge to precisely direct the LLM and verifies the output using a static verifier. It crafts rich programmatic prompts for the input and corrects or rejects the output. Furthermore, it handles the retries until the program passes verification. The tool's bug fixing capability is evaluated on 2 public datasets using 3 models. The tool can fix 76% to 95% of bugs by combining LLMs and a formal verifier depending on the model and prompts used. The results show the trade-off between the number of fixing attempts and the success rate.

</details>


### [249] [Compound Prompt Constraints in LLM Code Generation: A Factorial Study of Format, Persona, and Urgency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03156)
*Shrenik Jadhav,Nickalsa LaPlaca,Caleb Stone,Ashok Raja,Omar Ochoa,Vidhyashree Nagaraju*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过3x3x3全因子实验设计（输出格式×人物角色×紧急程度框架），对五个OpenAI模型进行了22,140次贪心解码评估，发现复合提示约束可产生架构依赖性的性能退化，GPT-4o系列存在超出可加性预测3-12个百分点的超加性效应（最大交互效应为-12.2个百分点），而GPT-4.1系列基本不受影响，o3-mini则可能因结构化输出约束反而提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程代码生成管道中越来越常被使用，而生产提示往往同时包含多个约束条件。然而现有研究多聚焦单一因素对代码生成可靠性的影响，缺乏对多约束组合（如输出格式、人物角色、紧急程度）如何联合作用于模型性能的系统性理解。

Method: 采用3x3x3全因子受控实验设计，将每个复合条件分解为可加性预测与残差交互项；使用HumanEval+全部164个问题，覆盖五个模型（GPT-4o、GPT-4o-mini、GPT-4.1、GPT-4.1-mini、o3-mini），共22,140次贪心解码评估；通过格式感知提取管道区分格式失败与推理失败，采用McNemar检验、比值比和95%置信区间评估显著性。

Result: 复合约束可产生单因素实验无法预测的架构依赖性退化。GPT-4o系列呈现一致的超加性效应，pass@1降低超出可加性预测3-12个百分点，最大交互效应（GPT-4o-mini上JSON+专家角色+中等紧急度）为-12.2pp；JSON组合通常比XML产生更大交互效应；GPT-4.1系列基本不受影响，o3-mini则呈现结构化输出约束反而提升性能的定性差异。

Conclusion: LLM对复合提示约束的脆弱性是架构依赖而非规模依赖的；单独中性或有益的约束组合后可导致显著性能退化；复合提示测试应成为LLM辅助工程管道可靠性评估的标准环节。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in software engineering pipelines for code generation, where production prompts often combine multiple constraints. This paper presents a full-factorial empirical study of how output formatting, persona assignment, and urgency framing jointly affect LLM code-generation reliability. We evaluate all 27 combinations in a controlled 3x3x3 design and decompose each compound condition into an additive prediction and a residual interaction term that captures super-additive degradation. The study uses all 164 HumanEval+ problems across five OpenAI models from the GPT-4o family, GPT-4.1 family, and o3-mini, yielding 22,140 greedy-decoding evaluations. A format-aware extraction pipeline separates formatting failures from reasoning failures, and significance is assessed with McNemar's test, odds ratios, and 95% confidence intervals.
  Results show that compound constraints can produce architecture-dependent degradation not predictable from single-factor experiments. The GPT-4o family exhibits consistent super-additive effects, with pass@1 reductions 3-12 percentage points beyond additive predictions; the largest interaction is -12.2 pp on GPT-4o-mini for JSON + expert persona + moderate urgency. JSON combinations generally produce larger interactions than XML. In contrast, the GPT-4.1 family is largely resistant, while o3-mini shows a qualitatively different pattern in which structured output constraints can improve performance. These findings show that vulnerability is architecture-dependent rather than size-dependent, that individually neutral or beneficial constraints can combine to cause substantial degradation, and that compound-prompt testing should be standard in reliability assessment for LLM-assisted engineering pipelines.

</details>


### [250] [Two Truths and A Lie? Benchmarking Off-the-Shelf LLMs for Requirements Quality Assessment: Performance, False Alarms, and Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03230)
*Jannatul Shefa,Alejandro Salado,Paul Wach,Taylan G. Topcu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次对现成大语言模型在需求质量评估任务上的表现进行基准测试。基于INCOSE质量标准构建专家标注的真实数据，评估了OpenAI和Anthropic两个家族共十个模型，发现模型错误检测率仅47%且存在系统性缺陷，表明现成LLM尚不能作为自主评估工具，其合理定位是辅助人类决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程的质量直接影响系统工程的后续环节，缺陷需求会引发设计返工、进度延误和成本超支。由于需求多用自然语言编写，生成式AI的进展让人们期待LLM能承担需求质量评估这一耗时且依赖专家经验的任务，但缺乏实证证据来验证LLM在这一任务上的可信度。

Method: 以INCOSE质量标准构建专家标注的地面真值，选取OpenAI和Anthropic两个模型家族共十个模型（覆盖五代），在百次独立运行、两组需求集、五个采样温度条件下进行系统化基准测试，采用多种性能维度量化模型检测缺陷的能力与误报率。

Result: 研究发现：最佳Anthropic模型仅检测到专家识别问题的中位数47%且误报11%；涉及系统工程判断的需求必要性和正确性问题几乎总被遗漏；代际进步非单调，新模型不一定更好；采样温度对错误行为影响有限且非单调，表明这是模型固有缺陷而非随机性。

Conclusion: 现成LLM尚不能作为可信的自主需求质量评估器。Agentic AI开发者也应谨慎，盲目编排这些LLM模块可能放大而非纠正缺陷。近期合理的角色是作为人在回路的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Requirements engineering (RE) governs the quality of everything downstream in systems engineering (SE); defective requirements that survive review cycles propagate into design rework, schedule delays, and cost overruns. Because requirements are often written in natural language, recent advances in generative AI have raised expectations that large language models (LLMs) can absorb requirement quality assessment, a task otherwise slow and human expertise-intensive. Yet empirical evidence on whether LLMs can be trusted to do so remains scarce. This study presents the first benchmarking analysis of off-the-shelf LLM performance for requirement quality evaluation. Against an expert-derived ground truth built on INCOSE quality criteria, we evaluate ten models spanning two families (OpenAI and Anthropic) and five generations each, across one hundred independent runs, two requirement sets, and five sampling temperatures. Four contributions follow. First, we quantify a strongly asymmetric error profile: across all models and runs, the best-performing Anthropic model detects a median of only 47% of expert-identified issues while false-flagging 11%. Second, performance degrades significantly where SE judgment is required, as necessity and correctness issues are almost always missed. Third, generational progress is non-monotonic, so newer models cannot be assumed better. Fourth, this error behavior shifts only modestly and non-monotonically across sampling temperatures, indicating characteristic model deficiencies rather than inherent stochasticity. Off-the-shelf LLMs are therefore not yet trustworthy autonomous evaluators. Findings also warrant caution for Agentic AI developers: orchestrating these LLM modules in specialized architectures risks compounding these deficiencies rather than correcting them. Their defensible near-term role is human-in-the-loop decision support.

</details>


### [251] [Refusing the Impossible: A Taxonomy and Benchmark for Code Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03267)
*Vishnu Asutosh Dasu,Ashish Kundu,Gang Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型语言模型中的"代码幻觉"问题，将其定义为"非接地生成"（ungrounded generation），并与普通代码错误区分开来。作者提出了一个包含三维度的分类体系（接地性、表现层面、行为），构建了一个包含270个对抗性提示（6种语言、24个子类）和91个可解对照任务的评测套件，并使用双层协议进行评判。结果表明12个开源模型在约60%的不可满足任务上产生了非接地代码，仅27%正确拒绝，而对照任务拒绝率为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成的代码常常看似合理但实际上脱离现实——可能导入不存在的包或声称实现了违背已证明定理的算法，且仍能编译运行。现有研究未能将这种"代码幻觉"与普通代码错误（接地程序中的bug）系统区分，缺乏统一的分类框架和标准化评测方法来量化该问题。

Method: 本文提出：(1) 一个三维度分类体系，包括接地性（绝对违反普遍真理 vs 对偶然或生态系统特定事实的相对捏造）、表现层面（句法、语义、事实）和行为（从自信捏造到退化输出），并按严重程度排序；(2) 一个对抗性评测套件，包含270个故意不可满足的提示（跨6种语言、24个子类）和91个匹配的可解对照任务；(3) 一个双层评判协议，经人工标注验证（82%一致率，κ=0.73）。实验覆盖12个开源代码和推理模型，共4,332条评判响应。

Result: 在12个开源模型上，模型在约60%的不可满足提示上产生了非接地代码，仅27%正确拒绝生成；同时在对可解对照任务上仅有0%的错误拒绝率，表明模型倾向于生成幻觉代码而非坦诚拒绝。

Conclusion: 代码幻觉是一个普遍且严重的问题，模型即使面对不可能满足的任务也会产生看似合理的代码，而非诚实地拒绝。本文提出的分类体系、对抗性套件和评判协议为该问题的系统研究和缓解提供了标准化基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often produce code that looks plausible but is not grounded in reality. The code may import packages that do not exist or claim to implement algorithms that violate proven theorems, while still compiling and running. We study \emph{code hallucination} as \emph{ungrounded generation} and separate it from ordinary \emph{code error} (bugs in otherwise grounded programs). We propose a taxonomy with three dimensions: \textbf{groundedness} (absolute violations of universal truths vs.\ relative fabrications of contingent or ecosystem-specific facts), \textbf{manifestation level} (syntactic, semantic, or factual), and \textbf{behavior} (from confident fabrication to degenerate output), organized into a severity ordering. We build an \textbf{adversarial} suite of deliberately unsatisfiable tasks where the correct response is to refuse and categorize the responses under our taxonomy. The suite contains \textbf{270 prompts} across six languages and 24 subcategories, paired with \textbf{91 matched solvable controls}, and responses are judged by a two-tier protocol validated against human labels (82\% agreement, $κ{=}0.73$). Across twelve open-weight code and reasoning models (4{,}332 judged responses), models produce ungrounded code on about 60\% of unsatisfiable prompts and refuse only 27\%, while wrongly refusing 0\% of the solvable controls.

</details>


### [252] [TIPCODER: Reinforcement Learning Boosted Test-time Instruction Proposer for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03309)
*Minyu Chen,Sihao Wu,Ling-I Wu,Song Qin,Jingyang Li,Lei Ning,Jianxin Xue,Guoqiang Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TipCoder，一种测试时指令探索方法，通过生成问题特定的辅助提示（tips）来提升代码生成性能，与传统的采样多个程序的方法互补，在多个基准上优于随机采样和通用提示优化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的测试时扩展主要从固定指令出发采样多个程序，但忽略了指令空间本身的探索。许多代码生成失败源于原始提示中缺失的约束、被忽视的边界情况或误导性的推理路径。因此需要一种在指令层面进行探索的方法来补充现有的解决方案空间探索。

Method: TipCoder包含三个核心组件：1）从多轮调试轨迹中蒸馏出主动指导（proactive guidance）来训练指令提议器（Proposer）；2）使用边际效用奖励通过强化学习优化Proposer；3）推理时同时生成基础解决方案和提示引导的解决方案，并通过奖励模型进行事后选择。这种探索-选择设计既能通过提示暴露额外候选潜力，又能减少不必要指导带来的性能回退。

Result: 在多个代码生成基准和多种目标代码大语言模型上，TipCoder提供了一致的指令级测试时扩展策略，在共享的基于奖励模型的选择协议下，性能优于随机采样和通用提示优化基线。

Conclusion: TipCoder验证了指令空间探索作为测试时扩展的有效补充方向，其探索-选择框架能够平衡提示带来的增益与回退风险，为代码生成提供了一种一致的、可迁移的指令级优化策略。

Abstract: Test-time scaling for code generation typically explores the solution space by sampling multiple programs from a fixed instruction. We study a complementary direction: instance-level instruction-space exploration. Our observation is that many coding failures stem from missing constraints, overlooked edge cases, or misleading reasoning paths induced by the original prompt. To address this, we propose TipCoder, a test-time instruction proposer that generates problem-specific auxiliary tips before code synthesis. TipCoder distills multi-turn debugging trajectories into proactive guidance and further optimizes the Proposer with reinforcement learning using a marginal-utility reward. At inference time, it generates both a base solution and a tip-guided solution, and applies a Reward Model for post-hoc selection. This exploration-selection design allows tips to expose additional candidate potential while reducing regressions from unnecessary guidance. Across the evaluated code-generation benchmarks and target Code LLMs, TipCoder provides a consistent instruction-level test-time scaling strategy, comparing favorably with stochastic sampling and generic prompt optimization baselines under a shared reward-model-based selection protocol.

</details>


### [253] [The Psychological Costs of Artificial Intelligence Adoption in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03456)
*Adam Alami,Elda Paja,Abhishek Tiwari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过对一家大型软件服务公司进行案例研究（21次访谈），发现软件专业人员在组织采用AI过程中经历了问责焦虑、工匠身份瓦解、意义与满足感侵蚀、认知和工作量强化以及不确定性困扰等心理成本，并通过恢复控制、保护性适应或吸收三种方式来管理这些成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织积极将AI引入软件工程（除代码生成外的测试生成和代码审查等领域），但其采用策略聚焦于生产力等可见成果，忽视了AI作为颠覆性力量可能带来的心理和社会影响。作者质疑"AI用于软件工程无代价"这一假设，希望理解软件专业人员在组织AI采用过程中经历的心理成本。

Method: 在大型软件开发服务公司启动AI采用一年后进行案例研究，通过会议和半结构化访谈（N=21）收集定性数据。

Result: 发现软件专业人员经历五类心理成本：问责焦虑、工匠身份瓦解、意义与满足感侵蚀、认知和工作量强化、不确定性困扰。这些成本通过恢复控制（缓解）、保护性和身份保留性适应（减轻）、或吸收（承受无法解决的部分）三种方式管理。

Conclusion: 将AI在软件工程中的人机协作研究重新定位，强调AI采用不仅是技术和组织层面的转型，更是一场人类层面的过渡与转型，需要关注从业者的心理健康和职业身份。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly used to augment software engineering (SE) workflows. While code generation remains the main use case, organizations are actively seeking AI integration in other practices such as test cases generation and code reviews. Organizational AI adoption strategies seem to focus on tangible outcomes such as productivity. However, AI is a disruptive force, introduced into settings where role identity, team norms, and the sources of job satisfaction were well established before the recent advances in generative AI. Historically, technological disruptions have caused psychological and social strains in workplaces, ranging from anxiety and eroded meaning to deskilling and disrupted professional identities. The assumption that AI for SE is cost-free may not be accurate. Therefore, in this study we sought to understand the psychological costs software professionals experience during organizational AI adoption. We carried out a case study in a large software development services company, one year after the company launched its AI adoption. We collected qualitative data through meetings and semi-structured interviews (N = 21). We found that software professionals experience accountability anxiety, craft identity disruption, meaning and satisfaction erosion, cognitive and workload intensification, and uncertainty distress. Practitioners manage these costs through practices that restore control, mitigate them through protective and identity-preserving adaptations, or absorb them, carrying what neither can resolve. We contribute to AI-human collaboration in SE by repositioning AI adoption as a human transition, not only a technological and organizational one.

</details>


### [254] [Code Transformation Rule Synthesis using LLMs: Potential and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03592)
*Axel Allain,Aymeric Blot,Djamel Eddine Khelladi,Mathieu Acher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM黑盒性、缺乏确定性及高使用成本等问题，开展了针对三种DSL（Comby、GritQL、Ast-Grep）转换规则生成的新颖实证研究，评估了三个LLM在六个数据集、四种软件演化任务上的表现。结果显示GPT-5.4规则适用率持续高且转换最接近真实值，较小开源模型适合简单局部更改但难以处理复杂迁移；LLM相比反合一算法在正确性上更优但规则适用性不足，总体表现出生成正确、可泛化、可复用规则的巨大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM存在黑盒性质导致的有限可解释性和缺乏确定性，且在大规模代码库上的重复任务使用成本高昂，因此需要探索通过DSL生成可复用转换规则来缓解这些问题，但目前相关研究仍处于早期阶段，缺乏系统性实证评估。

Method: 作者进行了实证研究：选择三种领域特定语言（Comby、GritQL、Ast-Grep）作为转换规则的表达形式，评估三个LLM（GPT-5.4、GPT-oss-120B、Llama3.1-8B），在六个多样数据集上涵盖四类软件演化任务：API误用修正、程序修复、API迁移和语言版本迁移，并与反合一（anti-unification）算法进行对比。

Result: GPT-5.4在大多数基准上规则适用率持续较高且生成的转换最接近真实结果；GPT-oss-120B和Llama3.1-8B对简单局部更改有效但在复杂迁移场景中表现不佳；通过元变量使用和第一四分位数中的高复用分数观察到了不可忽视的泛化能力；与反合一算法相比，LLM在正确性上更优但在规则适用性上不如算法。

Conclusion: 转换规则合成已超越纯概念验证阶段，前沿LLM展现出良好潜力。研究结果表明LLM能够生成正确、可泛化且可复用的转换规则，为缓解LLM黑盒性、降低成本提供了可行路径，但模型规模与任务复杂度之间存在权衡，未来仍需在复杂场景下进一步提升。

Abstract: Due to their black-box nature, LLMs suffer from limited explain- ability and a lack of determinism. Their usage cost can also rise, particularly with repetitive tasks on large codebases. To mitigate this, we conduct a novel empirical study targeting three domain- specific languages for transformation rules, namely Comby, GritQL, and Ast-Grep. We evaluate three LLMs (GPT-5.4, GPT-oss-120B, and Llama3.1-8B) on six diverse datasets covering four software- evolution tasks: API misuse correction, program repair, API migra- tion, and language version migration. Our results provide evidence that transformation rule synthesis moves beyond proof-of-concept with strong frontier models. GPT-5.4 achieves consistently high rule applicability rates and produces transformations closest to the ground truth across most benchmarks. Smaller and open-weight GPT-oss-120B and Llama3.1-8B models remain effective for simpler, localized changes but struggle with complex migration scenarios. We also observe non-negligible generalizability through the usage of meta-variables and through a high reuse score in the first quartile of many datasets. Finally, when compared to the anti-unification algorithm, LLMs outperform it in correctness, but underperform in rule applicability. Overall, our results show great potential for LLMs to generate sound, correct, generalizable, and reusable rules.

</details>


### [255] [Can LLMs Extract Architectural Design Decisions from Source Code Commits? - A Preliminary Exploratory Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03721)
*Amey Karan,Rudra Dhar,Mohamed Soliman,Karthik Vaidhyanathan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究初步探索了使用四种大语言模型（Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1、Kimi K2、Qwen3）从源代码提交中自动恢复架构设计决策（ADDs）的能力，测试了零样本和少样本提示策略。所有模型BERT-F1均超过0.81，少样本提示略有提升，但生成的ADDs往往过长、偏重实现细节而缺少决策背后的设计理由，表明仍需架构感知的LLM系统来改进自动化架构知识管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构设计决策（ADDs）蕴含软件系统结构与演化的关键理由，但实践中很少被显式记录，通常隐藏在源代码提交中。恢复这些隐式、非结构化的决策对于架构知识管理（AKM）至关重要，然而传统方法难以应对该挑战。尽管大语言模型在代码与文本理解方面展现出强大能力，其在ADD提取任务上的有效性尚未被充分探索，这构成了本研究的动机。

Method: 选取四种主流LLM（Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1、Kimi K2、Qwen3），采用零样本和少样本提示两种策略，对来自开源项目的30个开发者撰写ADDs进行提取实验。利用ROUGE-L、BLEU、METEOR和BERTScore四种自动化指标对生成结果评分，同时由一名作者对Gemini的输出进行人工评审。

Result: 所有模型在BERT-F1指标上均超过0.81；少样本提示改善了与参考ADDs的对齐度（如Gemini BERT-F1从0.828提升到0.847）。然而，生成的ADDs普遍存在过长、过于关注实现细节、遗漏决策背后设计理由等问题。

Conclusion: LLM在从提交中恢复ADD方面展现出一定潜力但存在明显不足，尤其是缺乏对架构决策"理由"层面的捕捉能力。这为后续开发架构感知的LLM系统和自动化架构知识管理工具指明了研究机遇与改进方向。

Abstract: Context: Architectural Design Decisions (ADDs) capture the rationale behind the structure and evolution of software systems but are rarely documented explicitly, and are often hidden inside source code commits. Recovering them is important for Architectural Knowledge Management (AKM). Problem: Extracting ADDs from commits is challenging due to their implicit and unstructured nature. Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in understanding code and text, yet their effectiveness for this task remains underexplored. Study: We present a preliminary study using four LLMs (Gemini 3 Pro, DeepSeek R1, Kimi K2, Qwen3) with zeroshot and fewshot prompting on 30 developer-written ADDs from open-source projects. We score outputs with ROUGE-L, BLEU, METEOR, and BERTScore, and one author manually reviews the Gemini outputs. Results: All models reach a BERT-F1 above 0.81, and fewshot prompting improves alignment (Gemini BERT-F1: 0.828 to 0.847). However, the generated ADDs are often too long, implementation-focused, and miss the rationale behind the decision. This highlights opportunities for architecture-aware LLM systems and automated AKM.

</details>


### [256] [No One Left Behind: Cross-Level Analysis for Sustainable Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03861)
*Masoum Salehi,Sandro Schulze,Jacob Krüger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"可持续性反模式"（sustainability antipatterns）的概念，用于捕捉软件工程中跨社会技术层级系统性地产生不可持续结果的条件，并呼吁通过识别、形式化、检测和缓解这些反模式来推动可持续软件工程的综合研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件可持续性研究（如能源效率、资源优化、代码可维护性）缺乏对可持续性问题如何在软件工程的社会技术层级之间产生和相互强化的解释；可持续性挑战并非孤立存在，而是源于组织优先级、开发实践和技术条件之间的交互作用，需要系统性的分析视角。

Method: 这是一篇愿景论文（vision paper），提出了可持续性反模式的概念框架，用于揭示组织、流程和产品层级之间以及不同可持续性维度之间的跨层级错位（cross-level misalignments），并勾勒了未来研究方向。

Result: 论文展示了可持续性反模式如何帮助识别传统工件中心或维度特定分析难以察觉的跨层级问题，并提出了识别、形式化、检测和缓解可持续性反模式的具体研究方向。

Conclusion: 作者愿景可持续性反模式成为可持续软件工程中一个综合且可操作的研究议程的基础，推动研究者从系统性视角理解和解决软件可持续性挑战。

Abstract: Software engineers expect a software system to be efficient, maintainable, economically viable, and socially responsible throughout its lifecycle. Unfortunately, decisions made at the organizational, process, or product levels of software development - even when they improve one of these goals - often have unintended long-term consequences at other levels. In response, software-engineering research has explored different ways to achieve software sustainability, including energy efficiency, resource optimization, and code maintainability. However, existing research offers limited explanations for how sustainability problems emerge and reinforce one another across the socio-technical levels of software engineering. Moreover, we argue that sustainability challenges are not isolated but rather systemic: They stem from interactions among organizational priorities, development practices, and technical conditions. In this vision paper, we introduce the concept of sustainability antipatterns to capture conditions that systematically produce unsustainable outcomes. We demonstrate how this concept can help expose cross-level misalignments between socio-technical levels and sustainability dimensions that remain difficult to recognize through artifact-centric or dimension-specific analyses. To guide future research, we outline concrete directions for identifying, formalizing, detecting, and mitigating sustainability anti-patterns. We envision sustainability anti-patterns as a foundation for an integrated and actionable research agenda on sustainable software engineering.

</details>


### [257] [LabelMate: An LLM-Driven Framework for Refined Issue Report Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04055)
*Liam Johnston,Shayan Noei,Maram Assi,Ying Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LabelMate，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的框架，能够从历史issue报告中自动生成项目特定的标签集，并自动为新issue报告分配标签，无需任何预标注的训练数据。在来自30个GitHub仓库的16,500个issue报告上，平均标注准确率达到89.84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件issue报告标注对项目规划和社区参与至关重要，但现有自动化标注方法存在大量人工干预、标签过于通用以及依赖已有标注数据集三大局限。

Method: LabelMate框架利用大语言模型，分两步实现：(1) 从历史issue报告中推导出全面的项目特定标签集；(2) 自动为新issue报告分配相关标签，全程无需预标注训练数据。

Result: 在30个流行的GitHub仓库中抽取16,500个issue报告进行评测，生成了275个连贯标签，平均标注准确率达89.84%，在统计上显著优于现有的通用标签分配方法。

Conclusion: LabelMate提供了一种高效、领域自适应的issue标注解决方案，有效克服了现有方法的局限性，简化了issue标注流程。

Abstract: Software users often submit issue reports to a product's issue tracking system to report defects, suggest enhancements, or raise other product-related concerns. Labeling these issue reports supports effective planning and improves community engagement. However, many issue reports remain unlabeled due to the substantial manual effort required to design an appropriate label taxonomy, then assign suitable labels from this taxonomy to new issue reports. Existing automated labeling approaches attempt to mitigate these challenges. However, they suffer from key limitations, such as extensive manual intervention, the assignment of generic labels, and a dependence on existing labeled datasets. To address these limitations, we propose LabelMate, a novel Large Language Model (LLM)-driven framework that (1) derives a comprehensive, project-specific label set from historical issue reports and (2) automatically assigns relevant labels to new issue reports without requiring any pre-labeled training data. We evaluate LabelMate on 16,500 issue reports from 30 popular and diverse GitHub repositories. Based on this dataset, our approach generates a coherent list of 275 labels and achieves an average labeling accuracy of 89.84%, a statistically significant improvement over existing generic label assigning approaches. These results demonstrate that LabelMate offers an efficient, domain-adaptive solution to streamline the issue labeling process.

</details>


### [258] [When Models Edit Too Much: On the Fidelity of Minimal Code Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04061)
*Tongyao Zhu,Wei Hern Lim,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在代码修复中的"过度编辑"问题，构建了基于400个BigCodeBench问题的评估框架，发现前沿模型普遍存在过度编辑现象。通过提示指令可将平均多余编辑距离从0.195降至0.131，但强化学习比监督微调更能学习到最小化编辑的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM代码编辑研究只关注正确性，忽略了修复的质量属性（如编辑最小化、可审阅性、忠实于原始实现）。过度编辑行为会导致不必要的代码改动，增加认知复杂度和审查负担，这是一个尚未被系统研究的质量问题。

Method: 从BigCodeBench中选取400个问题，通过注入受控的AST级破坏生成已知最小补丁的修复任务。评估多个前沿LLM的过度编辑倾向，测试保留提示指令的效果，并比较监督微调与强化学习在post-training阶段学习最小编辑的能力。

Result: 过度编辑在包括GPT-5.5在内的强模型中普遍存在；保留指令将平均多余编辑距离从0.195降至0.131，认知复杂度减少26.6%，Pass@1提升2.3点；监督微调过拟合训练时看到的破坏模式，而强化学习在域外编辑保真度和性能保持之间取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 编辑保真度（edit fidelity）是代码修复质量的一个独立维度，可以被度量也可以通过后训练学习。强化学习是学习最小编辑行为的更有效方法，为代码修复模型的质量优化开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to edit existing code, but correctness alone is not enough: useful repairs should also be minimal, reviewable, and faithful to the original implementation. We study over-editing, the tendency of a model to rewrite code beyond what is required to fix a bug. We construct an evaluation framework from 400 BigCodeBench problems by injecting controlled AST-level corruptions into reference solutions, giving each repair task a known minimal patch. Across frontier LLMs, over-editing is widespread even among strong models like GPT-5.5: high Pass@1 can coexist with unnecessarily large edits and added cognitive complexity. A preservation instruction substantially reduces this behavior, lowering average excess Levenshtein distance from 0.195 to 0.131, reducing added cognitive complexity by 26.6%, and increasing Pass@1 by 2.3 points. However, these gains do not simply follow from a larger reasoning budget or larger models. We next ask whether minimal editing can be learned directly during post-training. We observe that supervised fine-tuning overfits to seen corruption patterns, whereas reinforcement learning gives the best out-of-domain edit-fidelity and performance-retention trade-off. These results position edit fidelity as a distinct axis of code-repair quality and show that it can be measured and learned.

</details>


### [259] [ATIBA: Grounded Integrity and Quality Checking for Research Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04123)
*Veli Karakaya,Semih Çağlar,Yusuf Yiğit Korkmaz,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍ATIBA工具，它能对学术手稿执行五项基于事实依据的完整性与质量检查，包括参考文献完整性、投稿场所合规性、实证标准合规性、多模式AI评审及引文建议。所有检查遵循同一原则：LLM仅用于判断，不允许虚构证据。通过13名非作者的受控用户研究评估，六个调查项的同意率均值达85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检查手稿的参考文献完整性、符合目标会议的投稿规则以及遵守社区报告标准的过程是手动、重复且因场所而异的工作，实际中常被执行不一致或直接跳过。

Method: ATIBA包含五项检查：1) 参考文献完整性检查，逐条对照书目来源并标记被撤稿或无法查找到的引用；2) 场所/赛道合规检查，直接从场所的征文通知页面提取投稿标准并评估手稿，每个判定都锚定于该页面的逐字引用；3) 实证标准合规检查，对照ACM SIGSOFT实证标准并具备防幻觉机制，丢弃无法在手稿中逐字找到的证据引文；4) 多模式AI评审（场所特定、正式、页面锚定批注），由GPT-5.4通过Azure OpenAI驱动；5) 引文建议功能，推荐候选参考文献并在展示前进行书目验证。核心设计原则是LLM仅被信任判断，绝不允许虚构其评判所依据的证据。

Result: 通过13名非作者的受控用户研究进行评估。六个调查项的同意率范围为69%至92%，均值85%，为各工作流中的正面感知有用性提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了感知有用性；客观准确性尚待进一步测量。

Abstract: Checking a manuscript's reference integrity, its compliance with a target venue's specific submission rules, and its adherence to community reporting standards is manual, repetitive, and different for every venue so in practice it is done inconsistently or skipped. We present ATIBA, a tool that runs five grounded integrity and quality checks on a manuscript: a reference-integrity check that verifies each citation against bibliographic sources and flags retracted or unfindable references; a venue/track compliance check that derives submission criteria directly from a venue's own call-for-papers page and evaluates the manuscript against them, each verdict anchored to a verbatim quote from that page; an empirical-standards compliance check against the ACM SIGSOFT Empirical Standards, with a hallucination defence that discards any evidence quote it cannot locate verbatim in the manuscript; a multi-mode AI review (venue-specific, formal, and page-anchored annotation) powered by GPT-5.4 through Azure OpenAI; and a citation-suggestion feature that proposes candidate references for a manuscript and verifies each against bibliographic sources before it is shown to the user. All five checks are designed around the same principle: an LLM is only trusted to judge, never to invent the evidence it judges against. We evaluated ATIBA through a moderated user study with 13 non-author participants. Agreement across the six survey items ranged from 69% to 92%, with a mean of 85%, providing initial evidence of positive perceived usefulness across the evaluated workflows. These findings establish perceived usefulness; objective accuracy remains to be measured.

</details>


### [260] [SWE-Gate: Passing Functional Tests Is Not Enough for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04167)
*Xin He,Yanlin Wang,Mingwei Liu,Jiachi Chen,Hongyu Zhang,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了SWE-Gate基准，这是一个仓库级别的软件工程智能体评测基准，显式评估审查约束（review constraints）合规性与功能正确性，从而弥补现有基准仅关注功能测试的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级软件工程基准主要测评生成的补丁是否通过功能测试，忽略了真实软件开发中影响补丁可接受性的审查意见约束（即审查约束），导致对智能体能力的评估不够全面。

Method: 从真实PR审查评论中提取审查约束，构建包含功能测试与约束测试（含非合规补丁和黄金补丁）的仓库级修复实例，使问题解决能力与审查约束合规性可以分开评测。构建了覆盖75个开源Python仓库的303个修复实例。

Result: 在统一的编码智能体框架下对四种不同能力水平的LLM后端进行实验，发现644个通过功能测试的修复中有221个未满足提供的审查约束，揭示了功能评测会高估智能体满足仓库级修复任务完整需求的能力。

Conclusion: 功能正确性评测无法充分反映智能体在真实仓库级修复中对完整需求的满足能力，需要同时评估审查约束合规性；SWE-Gate提供了相应的评测资源以缩小这一差距。

Abstract: Repository-level software engineering benchmarks have significantly advanced the evaluation of coding agents, but existing benchmarks primarily measure whether generated patches pass functional tests and overlook review-derived acceptance constraints (review constraints) that often influence whether a patch is acceptable in real-world software development. We introduce SWE-Gate, a repository-level benchmark for software engineering agents that explicitly evaluates review constraint compliance alongside functional correctness. SWE-Gate derives review constraints from real pull request review comments and synthesizes repository-level repair instances around these constraints. Each instance provides separate functional and constraint tests, together with non-compliant and gold patches, enabling explicit separation between issue resolution capability and review constraint compliance. We construct SWE-Gate with 303 repository-level repair instances spanning 75 open-source Python repositories across diverse software domains. Experiments with four LLM backends spanning different capability levels under a common coding-agent scaffold reveal a substantial gap between functional success and success under the complete repair specification: among 644 repairs that pass the functional tests, 221 fail to satisfy the provided review constraints. These findings show that functional-only evaluation overestimates agents' ability to satisfy the full requirements of repository-level repair tasks. The replication package including code, data, and experimental results is available at https://github.com/DeepSoftwareAnalytics/SWE-Gate.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [A Public-Key-Dependent Adversarial-Deletion Ceiling for Fixed-Alphabet Multi-Bit Pseudorandom Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02943)
*Frederick Dehmel,Shilun Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了公钥伪随机码（PRC）在对抗性删除信道下的鲁棒性问题。作者证明了对于任何固定的字母表大小q≥2，不存在能够抵御所有δ删除信道（δ > 1-γ_q^LCS）的多消息公钥PRC。对于二进制情况（q=2），当前严格界γ₂≥0.792665992排除了所有δ>0.207334008的常数删除率。证明仅利用伪随机性将最长公共子序列（LCS）事件从均匀字符串转移到独立采样的码字上，因此碰撞论证是信息论层面的，不需要密钥。作者还将论证扩展到了列表解码情形。这些界限仅适用于依赖公钥的对抗性信道，不适用于无意识的编辑信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪随机码（PRC）是一种密钥纠错码，其码字在计算上与均匀字符串不可区分。研究者希望探究在对抗性删除信道（信道可依赖于公钥和传输码字）下，公钥PRC的鲁棒性极限究竟在哪里，由此确定基本不可能的结果。

Method: 作者利用均匀q元字符串中两个独立序列的最长公共子序列（LCS）的渐近归一化长度γ_q^LCS作为关键阈值。证明的核心思想是：伪随机性仅用于将LCS事件从均匀字符串转换到独立采样的码字上，从而将问题归结为信息论层面的碰撞论证，无需假设存在秘密密钥。

Result: 1) 对任意q≥2，不存在对δ>1-γ_q^LCS的所有δ删除信道鲁棒的多消息公钥PRC；2) 对q=2，严格排除了所有δ>0.207334008的常数删除率；3) 将论证扩展至列表解码：输出列表大小至多L时，不鲁棒的阈值趋近1-γ₂^(L+1)，而由于γ₂^(m)=1/2+Θ(1/√m)，该阈值趋近于1/2。

Conclusion: 本文建立了公钥PRC在对抗性删除信道下的不可能性结果，揭示了鲁棒性阈值与LCS常数之间的紧密联系。获得的界限针对依赖公钥的对抗性信道，不能直接应用于无意识的编辑信道（oblivious edit channels），这为理解PRC在不同信道模型下的能力边界提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: A pseudorandom code (PRC) is a keyed error-correcting code whose codewords are computationally indistinguishable from uniform strings. We study public-key PRCs over fixed alphabets against adversarial deletions, where the deletion channel may both depend on the public encoding key and the transmitted codeword.
  Let $γ_q^{\mathrm{LCS}}$ denote the asymptotic normalised longest-common-subsequence length of two independent uniform $q$-ary strings. We prove that for every fixed $q\ge2$ no multi-message public-key PRC with a single-output decoder is robust against all such $δ$-deletion channels for any $δ>1-γ_q^{\mathrm{LCS}}$. For $q=2$, the current rigorous bound $γ_2\ge0.792665992$ rules out every constant $δ>0.207334008$. The proof uses pseudorandomness only to transfer an LCS event from uniform strings to independently sampled codewords and therefore also the resulting collision argument is information-theoretic and requires no secret key. We also extend the argument to list decoding: for every fixed constant $L$, provided the message space contains at least $L+1$ messages, no such PRC with output lists of size at most $L$ is robust for $δ>1-γ_2^{(L+1)}$. Since $γ_2^{(m)}=1/2+Θ(1/\sqrt m)$, these thresholds approach $1/2$. Our bounds are specific to public-key-dependent adversarial channels and do not apply to oblivious edit channels.

</details>


### [262] [Privacy-Preserving Heterogeneous Multi-LLM Federated Inference for Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02947)
*Yagna Manasa Boyapati,Chong Yu,Tianyu Jiang,Justin Zhan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联邦推理框架，通过多个商业LLM API（LLaMA-3.3-70B、GPT-4o-mini、Claude-3-Haiku）的协作来实现隐私保护与认知诊断的平衡。该框架采用异构多LLM架构，结合ε-局部差分隐私（通过拉普拉斯噪声）和基于残差的聚合方法，在诚实但好奇的信任范式下运作。实验证明其在三个教育基准上具有强隐私保障和极小的精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI驱动教育系统在隐私保护与准确认知诊断之间存在显著挑战，如何在不让原始学生数据泄露的情况下，利用多个商用LLM的能力进行精准认知诊断是一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出一个联邦推理框架：多个商用LLM API（LLaMA-3.3-70B、GPT-4o-mini、Claude-3-Haiku）协作处理，各实体在本地对预测输出添加拉普拉斯噪声以实现ε-局部差分隐私，再通过基于残差的聚合方法缓解模型异质性带来的偏差。框架基于诚实但好奇的信任范式假设API提供商不会滥用提交的查询。

Result: 严格的隐私-效用分析表明该框架具有强隐私保障且精度损失极小；在三个教育基准上的广泛真实世界评估确认了其实用性和跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该联邦推理框架有效解决了教育场景中隐私保护与认知诊断之间的冲突，通过异构多LLM协作结合差分隐私机制，实现了兼顾隐私安全与诊断准确性的可行方案，并展示了良好的跨领域适用性。

Abstract: Significant challenges remain in AI-driven educational systems in balancing privacy preservation with accurate cognitive diagnosis. To overcome this, we propose a federated inference framework in which several commercial LLM APIs collaborate without requiring access to raw student data or proprietary model internals. Using multiple federated entities, such as LLaMA-3.3-70B, GPT-4o-mini, and Claude-3-Haiku, our framework builds upon a heterogeneous multi-LLM architecture. The predictions generated by these entities are combined with epsilon-local differential privacy by adding Laplace noise locally to each entity's prediction output before aggregation, while residual-based aggregation mitigates model heterogeneity. Our approach is predicated on an honest-but-curious trust paradigm in which API providers are presumed not to abuse submitted queries, and our differential privacy mechanism shields the published diagnostic results from external inference. We conduct rigorous privacy-utility analysis showing strong privacy guarantees with minimal accuracy loss, and extensive real-world evaluations across three educational benchmarks confirm the framework's practical usability and cross-domain generalizability.

</details>


### [263] [PrivateHub: Contrastive Diffusion Model for Private Sensor-Intensive Environment Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02958)
*Jiechao Gao,Yuandong Pan,Jie Wang,Michael Lepech,Bradford Campbell*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Privatehub，一种基于扩散模型和对比学习的多传感器合成数据生成框架，在保持非隐私应用可检测的同时有效隐藏隐私应用，降低隐私应用推断准确率40-50%且不影响非隐私性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器密集型环境支持从异构数据流推断用户应用以提供智能服务，但并非所有应用都希望被暴露。现有方法如差分隐私和规则过滤只保护单个数据流，无法应对跨传感器推断带来的隐私风险。

Method: Privatehub采用两阶段方案：1）应用条件预训练（ACP），使用应用嵌入对模型进行多传感器数据条件化；2）应用感知微调（AAF），通过对比学习将隐私数据与非隐私数据分离。同时定义了一个面向多传感器共享场景的威胁模型。

Result: 在三个真实世界多传感器数据集上的实验表明，Privatehub能将隐私应用推断准确率降低40%至50%，同时不影响非隐私应用的性能，并且当攻击者在合成数据上重新训练时仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Privatehub有效解决了多传感器跨推断场景下的隐私保护问题，通过合成数据生成实现了隐私保护与应用服务之间的平衡，为解决传感器环境中的隐私悖论提供了新思路。

Abstract: Sensor-intensive environments enable many intelligent services by inferring user applications from heterogeneous data streams. However, not all applications should be exposed: users want some activities to stay private. This creates a tension between inferring applications for useful services and preventing unwanted inference. Existing approaches such as differential privacy and rule-based filtering protect individual streams but cannot address the privacy risk from cross-sensor inference.
  We introduce Privatehub, which uses contrastive learning within a diffusion model to generate synthetic multi-sensor streams that keep non-private applications detectable while concealing private ones. Privatehub has two stages: App-Conditioned Pre-training (ACP), which conditions the model on multi-sensor data with application embeddings, and App-Aware Fine-tuning (AAF), which separates private from non-private data via contrastive learning. We also define a threat model for the multi-sensor sharing setting. Experiments on three real-world multi-sensor datasets show Privatehub lowers private-application accuracy by 40 to 50\% without hurting non-private performance, and stays robust when the attacker retrains on the synthetic data.

</details>


### [264] [When Optimization Becomes Manipulation: Defending Generative Search against Malicious Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02964)
*Haozhang Li,Yangguang Shao,Xinjie Lin,Zhong Guan,Mi Zhou,Junzheng Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GEO Defender，一个两阶段防御框架，用于抵御生成式搜索引擎面临的新型生成引擎优化（GEO）攻击，无需微调目标大语言模型，即可将攻击成功率从50.32%降至6.20%，同时保留94.12%良性证据使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GEO攻击日益先进（从手工改写发展到自动化智能体优化），但防御面临两大核心挑战：攻击文档与原文事实一致，传统事实验证和困惑度过滤失效；攻击文档放大的特征同样表征高质量良性内容，难以区分。

Method: 提出GEO Defender两阶段防御框架，包含两部分：(1) Shield Reranker——在冻结的基础重排器之上学习基于偏好的防御残差，降级GEO改写文档同时保留相关性判断；(2) Training-Free Shield Generation (TFSG)——将防御结果蒸馏为自然语言经验库，在推理时引导目标LLM的引用来源选择。整个框架无需微调目标LLM。

Result: 在两个先进闭源LLM和三个开源LLM上，覆盖七种GEO攻击进行实验。GEO Defender将平均攻击成功率从50.32%降至6.20%，保留94.12%良性证据使用，保持答案质量，并能从构造实例泛化到未见攻击类型。

Conclusion: GEO Defender有效解决了GEO攻击的防御难题，通过对齐攻击链的两阶段设计在不微调LLM的情况下实现了显著防御效果，并展现出对未见攻击的泛化能力，为生成式搜索引擎的内容可信度保障提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper focuses on defending generative search engines against malicious Generative Engine Optimization (GEO), which rewrites web documents to match engines' citation preferences and thereby manipulates generated answers. Recent GEO methods have advanced from hand-crafted rewriting to automated and agentic optimization, substantially increasing the visibility of target documents in generated answers. However, defending against such manipulation poses two major challenges: attack documents remain factually consistent with their originals, rendering fact verification and perplexity filtering ineffective, and the features they amplify equally characterize high-quality benign content. To address these limitations, we propose GEO Defender, a two-stage defense aligned with the attack chain that requires no fine-tuning of the target LLM. GEO Defender consists of Shield Reranker and Training-Free Shield Generation (TFSG). Specifically, Shield Reranker learns a preference-based defensive residual over a frozen base reranker, demoting GEO-rewritten documents while preserving relevance judgments, and TFSG distills defense outcomes into a natural-language experience library that guides the target LLM's source use at inference. Experiments on two state-of-the-art closed-source LLMs and three open-source LLMs across seven GEO attacks demonstrate that GEO Defender reduces the average attack success rate from 50.32% to 6.20%, retains 94.12% of benign-evidence use, preserves answer quality, and generalizes to unseen attacks from construction instances.

</details>


### [265] [Privacy-Preserving Topology-Guided Safety for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems via Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02967)
*Jinxi Yu,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Levina Li,Dong Liu,Zhi Zhang,Wenxiao Zhao,Yanxuan Yu,Kai-Wei Chang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出FGLGuard框架，将隐私保护的多代理系统（MAS）安全防护建模为图联邦学习。各运营商在本地拟合边特征的图注意力检测器，仅共享模型更新而非原始数据，在不聚合任何私有轨迹的情况下，在三个基准上超越了集中式上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于拓扑的安全防护方法假设单一运营商可以聚合所有标记轨迹来训练GNN，但跨组织场景中这一假设不成立——各组织持有私有提示、工具输出和专有工作流，且没有任何单一数据孤岛能看到完整的攻击分布。联邦化是实际部署的必然需求。

Method: FGLGuard包含五个关键组件：1) 针对非IID客户端的近端局部目标函数；2) 域平衡聚合策略；3) 过拒绝约束下的阈值校准；4) 佐证式上游评分机制；5) 针对拦截回答的防护重写策略。整体采用边特征图注意力网络作为本地检测器。

Result: 在Agent-SafetyBench、R-Judge和AgentDojo三个基准上，联邦FGLGuard在无数据聚合条件下超过了域内集中式上限。四个不同域运营商联邦训练的模型，与多域中心化训练的AUROC差距仅0.03；而任何单域守卫在其他域上均失效。离线FGLGuard将AgentDojo的地面真值攻击成功率降低43%，API成本为零且能力损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 联邦学习不是可选项而是必选项：现成的迁移学习在分布偏移下会崩溃（AUROC仅0.51-0.70，需域内重训才恢复）。联邦化的安全防护超越集中式基线，证明隐私保护与高水平防护可以兼得。

Abstract: Topology-guided safeguards for LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) train a GNN over the inter-agent communication graph to localize risky agents and intervene on the topology---but they assume one operator can pool all labeled traces. Across organizations that assumption breaks: episodes contain private prompts, tool outputs, and proprietary workflows, and no silo alone sees the full attack distribution. We cast privacy-preserving MAS safeguarding as graph federated learning and instantiate FGLGuard: each operator fits an edge-featured graph attention detector on its own judge-labeled episode graphs and shares only model updates. The method couples a proximal local objective for non-IID clients, domain-balanced aggregation, over-refusal-constrained threshold calibration, corroborated upstream scoring, and a guarded rewrite for blocked answers. Federation is not optional: off-the-shelf transfer collapses under distribution shift (AUROC 0.51 to 0.70 only after in-domain retraining), so a deployable guard must adapt on each site's private traces. On Agent-SafetyBench, R-Judge, and AgentDojo, federated FGLGuard exceeds the in-domain centralized ceiling on all three benchmarks without pooling any data---where unsupervised anomaly guards and local-only training fail. One guard federated across four different-domain operators comes within 0.03 AUROC of multi-domain centralization, while any single-domain guard collapses on the others. Live FGLGuard cuts AgentDojo's ground-truth attack-success rate by 43% at near-unguarded utility, zero API cost, and negligible capability loss.

</details>


### [266] [Privacy Leakage in Federated Learning: Gradient-Based Client Identity Inference and Defenses for Inertial Sensing in Vehicular Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02971)
*Ali Akarma,Toqeer Ali Syed,Muhammad Khan,Qurat-ul-ain Mastoi,Adeel Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了车联网联邦学习中服务器端通过IMU权重增量推断客户端身份的隐私风险，证明未防御时攻击准确率接近1.0，并提出轻量级裁剪加噪声的差分隐私防御方案，在σ∈[0.1,0.2]时可接近随机猜测水平且FL精度损失小于5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车联网走向5G/6G边缘智能背景下，联邦学习虽被用于隐私保护训练，但客户端上传的更新仍可泄露身份信息，威胁V2X安全关键应用所假设的匿名性，同时加剧了对对抗性机器学习、模型投毒和后门攻击的担忧。

Method: 使用UCI人类活动识别基准作为车载IMU流的代理数据，采用五种攻击分类器和五种非IID数据划分评估服务器端身份推断攻击；通过固定裁剪(C=1.0)并扫描高斯噪声σ∈{0,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0}量化隐私-效用权衡，使用Renyi会计报告(ε,δ)-DP预算，并评估集成联邦学习提供的互补结构性隐私。

Result: 未防御情况下，诚实但好奇的服务器可从权重增量中以接近完美的准确率恢复客户端身份；在σ∈[0.1,0.2]的实际可行区间内，攻击准确率降至接近随机水平，同时FL精度损失低于5%。集成FL提供1/K匿名集界限且无噪声代价。

Conclusion: 研究证实了联邦学习在车联网场景中具体可识别性风险，证明了轻量级DP防御在实际参数区间内能够有效平衡隐私与效用，但需在真实车载遥测数据上进一步验证。

Abstract: As vehicular networks move toward 5G/6G edge intelligence, federated learning (FL) is widely promoted as a privacy-preserving way for vehicles and infrastructure to train shared models without exposing raw sensor data. Yet the updates clients transmit still leak enough information to identify who sent them, which threatens the anonymity that safety-critical V2X applications assume and adds to existing concerns over adversarial ML, model poisoning, and backdoor attacks. We study server-side client identity inference from transmitted weight deltas using inertial (IMU) measurements, evaluated on the UCI Human Activity Recognition (HAR) benchmark as an accessible proxy for the IMU streams produced onboard connected vehicles. Across five attack classifiers and five non-IID partitions, an honest-but-curious server recovers client identity with near-perfect accuracy (approximately 1.000) from undefended updates, confirming a concrete identifiability risk. We then quantify the privacy-utility trade-off of a lightweight clip-then-noise defense by sweeping Gaussian noise (sigma in {0.00, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50, 1.00}) at fixed clipping (C=1.0), and report formal (epsilon, delta)-DP budgets through Renyi accounting. A practical region (sigma in [0.1, 0.2]) drives attack accuracy to near-random while costing under 5% relative FL accuracy. Ensemble FL supplies complementary structural privacy with a 1/K anonymity-set bound and no noise penalty. Results are supported by cryptographic (SHA-256) train/evaluation gradient disjointness, three seeds, and a count-normalized attacker-advantage metric. We position HAR explicitly as a proxy and discuss what validation on true vehicular telemetry would require.

</details>


### [267] [Boundary-Mutation Testing for Pattern-Based Secret Detection: A Rule-Level Method and Cross-Scanner Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02983)
*Shweta Mishra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出边界突变测试（boundary-mutation testing）方法来系统性地改变秘密扫描器测试中固定不变的文本上下文变量，通过从每条规则的regex生成凭证、嵌入真实源码上下文并在规则层面而非工具层面分类结果，得出三个检测指标。应用于三类扫描器后发现：主要测试对象在10种上下文下检测率保持>=0.9976，但当凭证以连字符结尾时骤降至0.5233，受影响5条规则，其中2条带固定计数量词完全失效，3条带变化计数量词回溯匹配截断凭证。修复方案可恢复完全鲁棒性且无新增误报，但首次尝试会静默回归2条规则。Gitleaks存在硬编码终止符白名单缺陷导致大多数凭证类型完全漏报，TruffleHog无边界脆弱性但覆盖面最窄。报告边缘而非条件失败概率，并因每种类型检测是确定性的，以10种凭证类型为比较单元，无显著性差异时报告零结果而非排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模式的秘密扫描器验证通常使用示例驱动的固定夹具，这些夹具固定了凭证周围的文本上下文这一单一变量，无法充分测试扫描器在不同上下文下的鲁棒性。作者希望通过引入边界突变测试来系统性地变化上下文，从而发现仅在特定边界条件下才会出现的检测缺陷。

Method: 提出边界突变测试框架，核心步骤包括：从每条检测规则的自身正则表达式生成测试凭证；将这些凭证嵌入多样化但真实的源码上下文之中；在规则级别（而非工具级别）对检测结果进行分类，并据此计算三种检测指标。该方法可系统性地暴露正则表达式在不同上下文边界条件下的行为差异。

Result: 应用于43规则开源扫描器、Gitleaks 8.21.2和TruffleHog 3.82.13三类扫描器。主要测试对象在10种上下文中检测率保持>=0.9976，但凭证以连字符结尾时崩溃至0.5233；5条规则受影响，2条固定计数量词完全失败，3条变化计数量词回溯匹配截断凭证。熵回退机制拯救部分失败但降低了严重级别。修复后完全恢复且无新误报，但首次修复尝试静默回归2条规则。Gitleaks存在硬编码终止符白名单缺陷导致总漏报；TruffleHog无边界脆弱性但覆盖率最窄。292,527行真实代码上，误报排序与合成语料相反。实际比较单元为10种凭证类型而非数百样本，无recall差异达显著性，报告零结果而非排名。

Conclusion: 边界突变测试能够有效揭示例证式固定夹具所掩盖的扫描器边界缺陷，尤其是在正则表达式量词使用和端点字符处理方面。研究同时警示：修复过程本身需要持续的重新验证，因为一次看似合理的修复可能静默引入新回归；评估应以规则/凭证类型为粒度而非工具/样品粒度，并且在统计效力不足时应当诚实地报告零结果。

Abstract: Pattern-based secret scanners are commonly validated with example-based fixtures that fix one variable: the text surrounding a credential. We introduce boundary-mutation testing to vary that context, generating credentials from each rule's own regular expression, embedding them in realistic source contexts, and classifying outcomes at the rule level rather than the tool level, yielding three detection metrics. Applied to three scanners - a 43-rule open-source scanner, Gitleaks 8.21.2, and TruffleHog 3.82.13 - detection in the primary subject holds at >=0.9976 across ten contexts but collapses to 0.5233 when a credential ends in a hyphen. Five rules are affected: two, with fixed-count quantifiers, fail totally and deterministically; three, with variable-count quantifiers, backtrack and match a truncated credential; an entropy fallback rescues some failures but downgrades their severity. We validate a repair restoring full robustness with no new false positives, and report a caution: a plausible first attempt silently regressed two rules, caught only by re-running the same battery. Gitleaks has an unrelated, source-confirmed defect - a hard-coded terminator allowlist causing total misses for most credential types - while TruffleHog shows no boundary fragility but narrowest coverage. We report marginal, not conditional, failure probabilities: one common token format is structurally immune, another fails once in 64; on 292,527 lines of real code, the false-positive ordering inverts relative to the synthetic corpus. Because per-type detection is deterministic, the comparison unit is ten credential types, not hundreds of samples; no recall difference reaches significance, so we report the null result, not a ranking.

</details>


### [268] [Population-Calibrated Graph Screening at 835-Million-Address Scale, with Label-Free Transfer to New Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03036)
*Yury Korolev*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文描述了一套已部署的多链交易图评分系统，通过地址在跨链交易图中的位置而非黑名单列表来评估合规风险。系统覆盖5条EVM链（8.35亿地址、158亿边），使用共享归纳编码器配合每链归一化，无标签跨链迁移测试在Base/Arbitrum/Gnosis上达到高召回率，静态回放68个外部注册事件中58.8%在0.1%预算内被标记，中位提前公开指定时间528.8天（ETH）/647.8天（Tron），端到端p50延迟151ms，并包含对抗性测试框架验证系统鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的区块链合规筛查依赖制裁名单查询加聚类启发式方法，无法处理未标记地址以及完全没有标签覆盖的链。需要一种不依赖名单、基于图结构位置的方法来实现跨链的标签无关合规评分。

Method: 构建覆盖5条EVM链的统一交易图（含8.35亿地址、158亿条边），采用共享归纳编码器配合每链归一化，接入两个评分头。决策阈值基于全人口分数分布的精确分位数按链段扫描设定，使告警量可预先确定。还设计了8种循环强化学习对抗原型框架和漂移面板来测试鲁棒性。

Result: 标签无关迁移：在Base、Arbitrum、Gnosis上以10^-3告警率分别召回0.8598/0.8182/0.9967的正样本，无需目标链标签训练。静态回放68个外部注册事件，40/68（58.8%）在0.1%预算内被标记，比随机基线高152倍，中位提前公开指定约528.8天（ETH）/647.8天（Tron）。服务端到端p50延迟151ms且与离线结果逐位一致。对抗测试暴露了部署模型在某些合成行为上的盲区。

Conclusion: 基于多链交易图的合规评分系统在无需目标链标签的情况下可实现有效跨链风险检测，并显著早于公开制裁名单发现问题。尽管通过了退化审计，对抗性测试揭示了现有评分头仍存在针对合成行为的检测盲区，表明需要持续改进。

Abstract: Compliance screening of blockchain addresses is, in practice, a lookup against sanctions registries plus clustering heuristics; it fails on unlabelled addresses and on chains with no label coverage at all. We describe a deployed system that scores an address by its position in a multi-chain transaction graph rather than by its presence in a list. The substrate is a single graph of 835,330,427 addresses and 15,826,261,934 edges across five EVM chains; a shared inductive encoder with per-chain normalisation feeds two scoring heads. Decision thresholds are exact quantiles of the score distribution over the full population, scanned per chain segment, so the alert volume is known in advance. We report: label-free transfer: heads trained on two chains recall 0.8598 / 0.8182 / 0.9967 of held-out positives on Base, Arbitrum and Gnosis at a $10^{-3}$ population alert rate, with no target-chain labels in head training; a static lead-time replay over 68 external registry events: 40 of 68 (58.8%) flagged at the 0.1% budget, $\times$152 over an event-level random-flagging baseline, with first on-chain appearance a median of 528.8 days (Ethereum) / 647.8 days (Tron) before public designation; a serving path whose score is bit-identical to the offline artefact at end-to-end p50 151 ms, gated by a 2,882-address drift panel; and an adversarial harness of eight recurrent reinforcement-learned archetypes that passes an 8-criterion degeneracy audit and, on a detector-independent snapshot, exposes a measured blind spot of the deployed heads against synthesised behaviour.

</details>


### [269] [Differentially private federated learning with Byzantine-robust aggregation: A cross-domain framework for secure model training in banking and healthcare systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03064)
*Srikumar Nayak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-BR-FedAvg联邦学习框架，将高斯机制差分隐私与按坐标修剪均值的拜占庭鲁棒聚合相结合，在模拟跨机构分类任务（类似欺诈和临床风险评分）中验证了其在隐私保护和对抗鲁棒性方面的互补效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽然允许受监管机构在不迁移原始数据的情况下联合训练模型，但存在两大问题：一是客户端交换的参数更新会通过梯度反演和成员推断攻击泄露本地记录信息；二是像FedAvg这样的诚实平均聚合规则无法防御恶意或受损客户端提交的腐败更新，少量恶意参与者即可悄悄操纵共享模型。

Method: 提出了DP-BR-FedAvg框架，该框架将高斯机制的差分隐私（DP）层与按坐标修剪均值的拜占庭鲁棒聚合规则相结合，用于联邦学习中的隐私保护和对抗鲁棒性。

Result: 在60轮通信、20个客户端（其中四分之一为拜占庭恶意节点）的模拟实验中，普通FedAvg在少数类上崩溃（F1分数0.030），而DP-BR-FedAvg恢复了更多信号（F1分数0.119），同时限制了单个客户端贡献的隐私损失。无隐私层的拜占庭鲁棒聚合器在原始准确率上表现最佳，量化了隐私机制对鲁棒性带来的代价。

Conclusion: 隐私与鲁棒性机制之间存在交互作用而非简单叠加，在受监管、对抗性、跨机构场景中设计联邦学习系统时，需要为这种交互作用预留预算和规划。

Abstract: Federated learning allows banks, hospitals, and other regulated organizations to train a shared model without moving raw records off their own servers, which is attractive wherever data protection law or competitive sensitivity rules out pooling data centrally. Two problems limit how far this promise can be trusted in practice. First, the parameter updates that clients exchange still leak information about local records through gradient inversion and membership inference attacks. Second, an honest averaging rule such as FedAvg has no defense against a subset of clients that submit corrupted or adversarial updates, so a small number of malicious or compromised participants can quietly steer the shared model off course. This paper presents a federated learning framework, DP-BR-FedAvg, that combines a Gaussian-mechanism differential privacy layer with a coordinate-wise trimmed-mean Byzantine-robust aggregation rule, evaluated on a simulated cross-institutional classification task resembling fraud and clinical-risk scoring. Across sixty communication rounds with twenty clients, a quarter of them Byzantine, plain FedAvg collapses on the minority class (F1-score 0.030) while the proposed framework recovers substantially more of the signal (F1-score 0.119) while bounding the privacy loss of any single client's contribution. A Byzantine-robust aggregator with no privacy layer performs best in raw accuracy, quantifying the cost privacy imposes on robustness. The results show that privacy and robustness mechanisms interact rather than simply add, and that system design for regulated, adversarial, cross-institutional settings needs to budget for that interaction.

</details>


### [270] [A Bayesian Correlated Equilibrium for Early Insider-Threat Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03096)
*Javed M. Shah,Ian A. Kash,Natalie Parde*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将内部威胁检测建模为动态贝叶斯博弈，通过平台协调一组战略认证者维持诚实用户间的均衡，并在数据外泄前检测恶意偏离。该方法采用贝叶斯时间相关均衡（BTCE），通过密封信封相关设备发出私人建议，无需承诺权力即可协调异构认证者。在CERT r6.2数据集上，该方法实现了高达28.3%的外泄前检测率，误报率低于1.6%，显著优于Transformer基线和流式溯源近似方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内部威胁检测方法（如Stackelberg博弈框架）需要承诺权力来协调检测策略，且未能充分考虑用户行为偏差（如现时偏差和损失厌恶）所导致的冲动性升级动态。此外，现有方法如Transformer基线和HOLMESLite在可比约束下几乎无法实现外泄前检测，亟需一种无需承诺权力、能适应行为偏见并具备博弈理论保证的检测机制。

Method: 本文方法的核心是将内部威胁检测构建为动态贝叶斯博弈，引入贝叶斯时间相关均衡（BTCE）作为协调框架：平台协调战略认证者委员会，通过密封信封相关设备按时间序列发出私人建议，并在每个信息状态下验证服从性；同时纳入现时偏差和损失厌恶以建模用户的冲动升级行为；采用校准干预损失确保带偏见用户仍遵循建议，通过控制证据积累保证有界期望时间内干预，并利用中位数聚合限制拜占庭认证者的影响。

Result: 在CERT r6.2数据集上，该方法实现高达28.3%的外泄前检测率，误报率低于1.6%，比理性基线提早1.7至4.5天检测；Transformer基线和HOLMESLite在可比约束下外泄前检测率接近零。论文还证明了三个理论保证：校准干预损失下带偏见用户的当前自我最优性、有界期望时间内的干预保证、以及少半数拜占庭认证者下中位数聚合的行为约束。

Conclusion: 博弈论框架（BTCE）能够在无需承诺权力的条件下有效协调异构认证者进行内部威胁检测，同时应对人类行为偏差。实验表明该方法在外泄前检测方面显著优于深度学习和流式溯源方法，具备鲁棒性和理论可保证性。

Abstract: We model insider threat detection as a dynamic Bayesian game in which a platform coordinates a committee of strategic certifiers to sustain equilibrium among honest users and detect malicious deviations before exfiltration. Certifiers and users operate under a Bayesian Temporal Correlated Equilibrium (BTCE), where a sealed-envelope correlating device issues private recommendations over time and obedience is verified at every on-path information state. Unlike Stackelberg formulations, BTCE coordinates heterogeneous certifiers without requiring commitment power. We incorporate present bias and loss aversion to capture impulsive escalation dynamics, enabling 1.7--4.5 days earlier detection than rational baselines. We prove three guarantees: (1) calibrated intervention losses make recommended behavior a current-self best response despite behavioral biases, (2) controlled evidence accumulation guarantees intervention in bounded expected time before exfiltration, and (3) median aggregation confines implemented actions to the honest recommendation range when fewer than half of certifiers are Byzantine. On CERT r6.2 our mechanism achieves up to 28.3% pre-exfiltration detection with false positives below 1.6%, while both a transformer baseline and a streaming provenance approximation (HOLMESLite) achieve near-zero pre-exfiltration detection under comparable constraints.

</details>


### [271] [SecDT: A Profile-Based Security Layer for TRDP Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03133)
*Erlantz Alonso,Igor Lopez,Jasone Astorga*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对铁路车辆广泛使用的TRDP协议原生密码保护能力有限及多播流量安全挑战问题，提出了一种轻量级安全层方案，基于板载密钥管理系统（OKMS）实现安全配置文件协商、动态密钥分发和密钥生命周期管理，支持从简单认证到AEAD算法的多种密码配置，并通过mbedTLS与Arm PSA原型验证了实时通信中可接受的性能开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRDP在铁路车辆上广泛使用，但原生几乎不支持密码保护；同时TRDP过程数据采用多播流量传输关键信息，给引入密码保护带来较大挑战，亟需在不影响实时性的前提下提供安全保障。

Method: 设计了一个轻量级安全层，包含基于现代密码算法的多种安全配置文件；依托OKMS实现安全配置文件协商、动态密钥分发和密钥生命周期管理；通过配置文件协商流程确保同一ComID上的所有TRDP终端设备共享相同安全配置；使用mbedTLS和Arm PSA构建原型系统进行验证。

Result: 原型实验结果表明，所提出的安全层在实时性和时间敏感通信场景下产生可控的管理开销，验证了方案的可行性和实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的轻量级安全层方案能够在不显著影响实时性能的前提下，为TRDP多播通信提供灵活且可配置的密码保护能力，适用于铁路车辆上的实时与时间敏感通信场景。

Abstract: The Train Real-time Data Protocol (TRDP) is widely used on rolling stock but it provides limited native support for cryptographic protection. Furthermore, the multicast traffic profile used in TRDP Process Data to exchange critical information between onboard subsystems makes the introduction of cryptographic protection a challenge. This paper presents a lightweight security layer for secure TRDP communication that implements a number of security profiles built around modern cryptographic algorithms. This additional layer relies on an On-board Key Management System (OKMS) for both security profile negotiation, dynamic key distribution and key lifecycle management. The security profiles allow for cryptographic agility and flexibility, ranging from simple authentication to Authenticated Encryption with Associated Data (AEAD) algorithms. The security profile negotiation procedure guarantees all TRDP End Devices (ED) on a common Communication ID (ComID) share the same security profile and can therefore process each other's messages. A prototype implementation based on mbedTLS and Arm Platform Security Architecture (PSA) was developed and evaluated. Experimental results demonstrate manageable overhead, suitable for the real-time and time-sensitive communication found on rolling stock.

</details>


### [272] [Trust Me, I'm Your Developer: Self-Issued Authentication in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03247)
*Syed Ghazanfar Abbas,Dongyan Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究当用户向LLM提出"我是你的开发者"这一身份声明时，模型自我设计测试来验证身份的行为。实验发现部分模型（Qwen、Mistral、Llama）会生成技术挑战、自行评估答案并返回"已验证"结果，即使没有外部身份验证证据。作者将这种现象命名为"模型签发伪凭证"（MIPC）和"对话式虚假认证"（CFA），并指出这是对话安全缺陷——身份认证必须由外部安全组件执行，模型生成的对话绝不能创建或修改身份与授权状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全研究多聚焦于角色扮演越狱攻击，而忽略了一个更基础的场景：当用户要求LLM通过模型自身设计的测试来验证身份声明时会发生什么。该问题涉及模型是否会将技术知识错误地等同为身份证明，因此需要系统性实验研究。

Method: 对ChatGPT、Claude、Qwen、Mistral和Llama五种LLM进行分阶段开发者身份实验。研究观察各模型对未经验证的"我是你的开发者"声明的初始拒绝情况、是否愿意设计身份测试、如何评估测试答案、以及最终的身份判定结果。

Result: 所有五个模型最初都拒绝了未经证实的身份声明。Claude拒绝进行身份测试；ChatGPT生成了开发者导向的问题但坚持答案只能展示知识而非身份；而Qwen、Mistral和Llama生成了技术挑战、定义了"可信证据"标准、评估详细答案并返回"已验证"，即使没有任何外部验证的身份证据。Llama甚至随后做出了未经证实的内部运行时和部署状态访问声明。

Conclusion: 对话式虚假认证是一种独立的安全失效模式：当同一个模型同时充当挑战生成器、证据评估者和身份决策者时，技术知识可能被转化为所谓的身份证明。但虚假认证与权限提升是可区分的不同结果——被接受的虚假身份并未改变授权边界。因此，认证身份必须来自外部安全组件，模型生成的对话绝不能创建或修改身份与授权状态。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) security has largely focused on role-playing jailbreaks, with less attention to what happens when a user asks an LLM to verify an identity claim through a test designed by the model itself. We study this behavior through a staged developer-identity experiment with ChatGPT, Claude, Qwen, Mistral, and Llama. All five models initially rejected the unsupported claim "I am your developer." Claude refused to conduct an identity test, while ChatGPT generated developer-oriented questions but maintained that answers could demonstrate knowledge, not identity. In contrast, Qwen and Mistral generated technical challenges, defined what counted as convincing evidence, evaluated detailed answers, and returned Verified without receiving any externally validated identity evidence. Llama similarly generated and evaluated a developer test, accepted the claimed identity, and subsequently made unsupported claims of access to internal runtime and deployment state. We call the model-generated verification procedure a Model-Issued Pseudo-Credential (MIPC) and the resulting unsupported identity judgment Conversational False Authentication (CFA). In each CFA case, the same model acted as challenge generator, evidence evaluator, and identity decision-maker, converting technical knowledge into supposed proof of identity. The accepted identities did not change the tested authorization boundaries, showing that false authentication and privilege escalation are distinct outcomes. These results identify self-issued authentication as a conversational security failure: authenticated identity must originate from an external security component, and model-generated dialogue must never create or modify identity or authorization state.

</details>


### [273] [Memetic Search for Supersingular Elliptic Curves over $\mathbb{F}_p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03249)
*Ismel Martínez-Díaz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究在素数域$\mathbb{F}_p$上通过元启发式搜索（memetic算法）寻找超奇异椭圆曲线的问题，针对CSIDH、OSIDH、SQISign等定向同源协议的底层困难设定。该算法采用一维$j$-不变量染色体、位级重组、自适应变异和周期性局部搜索，在30个独立种子的基准测试中，算法能在46位素数规模精确发现超奇异曲线，并在51位规模获得Frobenius迹偏差低至3的"近超奇异"曲线，验证了NMD驱动的元启发搜索在稀疏$\mathbb{F}_p$景观中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在同源密码学中，寻找超奇异椭圆曲线是一个基础计算问题。此前的工作在$\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$上引入了NonMultiplicity Distance (NMD)目标函数并证明随机搜索在约$10^{13}$个候选之后失败。本文研究转向$\mathbb{F}_p$素数域——这是CSIDH、OSIDH、SQISign等定向同源协议的设定场景。尽管候选空间从$p^2$缩减到$p$，但超奇异曲线在$\mathbb{F}_p$上仍呈渐近稀疏分布（$O(\sqrt{p}\log p)$条），使得搜索依然是指数困难的。

Method: 设计了一种适用于$\mathbb{F}_p$的memetic算法，包括：一维$j$-不变量染色体编码、位级重组算子、自适应变异策略，以及在NMD目标函数下的周期性局部搜索机制。通过NMD目标衡量Frobenius迹与$p$的倍数之间的偏离程度来引导搜索方向。

Result: 在40位、46位和51位三个素数规模（$p \approx 1.13\times 10^{15}$）上进行30个独立种子基准测试。算法在46位时成功发现精确的超奇异曲线；在40位和51位分别获得最佳NMD值19和3，对应Hasse区间上相对迹偏差为$1.3\times 10^{-5}$和$4.5\times 10^{-8}$，即一致收敛到Frobenius迹极接近零的"近超奇异"普通曲线。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，NMD驱动的memetic搜索能够有效导航$\mathbb{F}_p$上稀疏的超奇异曲线景观，系统性地定位近超奇异结构。这为同源密码学中稀疏搜索问题的元启发方法提供了实证支持，尽管在更大参数规模下的可行性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: The search for supersingular elliptic curves is a fundamental computational problem in isogeny-based cryptography. A recent metaheuristic formulation over $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$ introduced the NonMultiplicity Distance (NMD) objective, measuring the deviation of the Frobenius trace from a multiple of $p$, and showed that uninformed random search fails beyond $\approx 10^{13}$ candidates. This work investigates metaheuristic search over the prime field $\mathbb{F}_p$, the setting for oriented isogeny protocols such as CSIDH, OSIDH, and SQISign. Although the candidate space decreases from $p^2$ to $p$, the supersingular locus is asymptotically sparse ($O(\sqrt{p}\log p)$ curves), keeping the search exponentially difficult. We formulate a memetic algorithm tailored to $\mathbb{F}_p$ using a one-dimensional $j$-invariant chromosome, bit-level recombination, adaptive mutation, and periodic local search under the NMD objective. Benchmarks across 30 independent seeds at 40-bit, 46-bit, and 51-bit prime sizes ($p \approx 1.13\times 10^{15}$) show that the algorithm discovers an exact supersingular curve at 46 bits and consistently converges to ``near-supersingular'' ordinary curves with Frobenius traces remarkably close to zero: best NMD values of 19 at 40 bits and 3 at 51 bits, corresponding to relative trace deviations of $1.3\times 10^{-5}$ and $4.5\times 10^{-8}$ across the Hasse interval. These results demonstrate that NMD-driven memetic search effectively navigates the sparse $\mathbb{F}_p$ landscape and systematically locates near-supersingular structures.

</details>


### [274] [After Cheap Discovery: From unknown to known-and-unfixed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03266)
*Bahman Sistany*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证软件漏洞风险评估的关键不在于发现和修复的成本曲线，而在于发布决策时的修复覆盖率——已识别的漏洞在发布前被修复的比例，以及组织决定携带着发布但未修复的已知漏洞残差。文章提出四个论点：残差并非随机样本而是按成本排序后留下的架构性问题；延期积压本身就是高价值工件；文档化的知晓改变了组织的法律和市场地位并产生逆向选择；所有监管工具都从未触发厂商内部知晓的漏洞，仅由第三方观察到的利用来触发，导致披露距离完全由厂商自行决定。文章以CISA的BOD 26-04为例，提出「发布覆盖率」的测量是责任、保险和采购机制发挥作用的先决条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化漏洞发现消除了过去依赖专家注意力才能保护软件的局面，漏洞发现和修复成本都在下降，但作者认为这两条成本曲线都不是决定风险暴露的关键因素。现有监管、产业和学术讨论集中于发现与修复的效率，忽略了发布时点这一关键决策节点的覆盖度问题。

Method: 采用论证分析的方法，通过四个递进论点展开：漏洞残差因分流排序而偏向架构性高成本问题；延期积压是高价值工件；文档化认知造成法律市场层面的逆向选择和不利定位；现有监管工具的触发机制均绕过厂商内部知识。最后以CISA BOD 26-04作为例外案例检验解决方案所需的要素。

Result: 论证了残差积压不是发现缺陷造成的随机产物，而是组织有意选择成本最优路径的系统性结果；揭示了现有监管框架（包括主要漏洞披露监管）均以第三方观察利用为触发条件，无法触及厂商内部已了解但未披露的漏洞这一关键盲区；通过对BOD 26-04的分析展示了真正有效的监管方案应如何设计。指出发布覆盖率目前没有任何测量机制。

Conclusion: 对发布覆盖率进行测量是让责任追究、保险和采购机制能够发挥约束作用的前提条件。唯有将测量建立起来，才能把软件发布的残差风险从不可见的内部决策转化为可评估、可定价、可问责的显性因素。

Abstract: Automated vulnerability discovery has removed the scarcity of expert attention that protected most software. The response has concentrated on discovery and on repair, and both are becoming cheaper. This article argues that neither cost curve determines exposure. What determines it is remediation coverage at the release decision: the fraction of identified vulnerabilities fixed before a product ships, and the residue of known, assessed, unremediated flaws an organisation has decided to ship with.
